##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: household.h_is_hoh
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household box checked in the Filing Status section"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}

  - id: household.h_not_married
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Unmarried on December 31, 2025 (never married, divorced under a final decree, legally separated under a decree, or widowed before 2025), and spouse did not die in 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.married_dec31, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.spouse_died_this_year, {const: false}]}
    note: "Pub. 501: if your spouse died during the year you are considered married for the whole year, so a spouse who died in 2025 rules out the unmarried branch even when married_dec31 is answered No."

  - id: household.h_considered_unmarried
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married at the end of 2025 but considered unmarried (married persons who live apart, or spouse a nonresident alien with no election)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.married_dec31, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.considered_unmarried, {const: true}]}

  - id: household.h_unmarried
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Unmarried (or considered unmarried) on the last day of the year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.h_not_married, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.h_considered_unmarried, {const: true}]}

  - id: household.h_test1_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of dependents meeting Test 1 (parent whose main home for all of 2025 you paid over half the cost of keeping up)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test1, {const: true}]}

  - id: household.h_test2_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons meeting Test 2 (qualifying person who lived with you for more than half of the year)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test2, {const: true}]}
    note: "Assumes dependent.hoh_test2 already applies the Test 2 exclusions (divorced-parent rule dependent, dependent only because the person lived with you all year, multiple support agreement) and the exception to time lived with you."

  - id: household.h_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 1 applies"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [household.h_test1_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  - id: household.h_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Test 2 applies: you paid over half the cost of keeping up a home in which you and a qualifying person lived for more than half of the year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.home_cost, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [household.h_test2_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}

  - id: household.h_qualifies
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the Head of household box only if you are unmarried (or considered unmarried) and either Test 1 or Test 2 applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.h_unmarried, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [household.h_test1, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.h_test2, {const: true}]}

  - id: household.hoh_fails
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household is checked but the filer is not unmarried (or considered unmarried) or neither Test 1 nor Test 2 applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [household.h_is_hoh, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          not: {op: test, eq: [household.h_qualifies, {const: true}]}
    note: "Interface meaning inferred from i1040gi Head of Household: true when the return claims head of household status and the stated requirements are not met; false when another status is claimed."

  - id: household.mfs_community_property
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing separately with community property: you, or your spouse, were domiciled in a community property state, or community pension income is received (Pub. 555; Form 8958 needed)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [household.community_property, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [household.community_property, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [household.spouse_cp_domicile, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.cp_retirement, {const: true}]}
    note: "Open: the pages say married persons domiciled in a community property state who file separately must split community income (Form 8958, Pub. 555). They also say each spouse's domicile must be checked, and that pensions or military/civil service retirement earned while married and domiciled in such a state may be community income even after moving. All three cases are counted. Registered domestic partners are not married and do not file MFS, so household.rdp is not used here."
```

Second transcription's note: Open: the pages say married persons domiciled in a community property state who file separately must split community income (Form 8958, Pub. 555). They also say each spouse's domicile must be checked, and that pensions or military/civil service retirement earned while married and domiciled in such a state may be community income even after moving. All three cases are counted. Registered domestic partners are not married and do not file MFS, so household.rdp is not used here.
